OPTIONS COMPRESS=YES;
LIBNAME CARTAO1    '/riscosbv/cartoes/PORTFOLIO';
LIBNAME CARTAO '/riscosbvf/financas/varejo/Cartoes';

LIBNAME MIS2 '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/fatigatte/MIS';

LIBNAME estudos2 '/riscosbvf/indicadores/Estudos_2';
LIBNAME CARNEIRO '/riscosbv/cobranca/jcarneiro/';
LIBNAME PG_CYBER	BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/jcarneiro/pagos_cyber';
LIBNAME WAG '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/wsilva';

%LET Data_Referencia = '30Sep2025'd; 
%LET Safra_Referencia = 202509; 

%LET mes1='09'; 
%LET ano1='2025'; 

%PUT &Data_Referencia.;
%PUT &Safra_Referencia.;
%PUT &mes1.;
%PUT &ano1.;

%macro ConexaoIQ(server);
	%let server = %upcase(&server.);
	%if &server. eq BI
	%then %do;	connect to sasiosiq as dbcon (host="iqprdbi" server=iqprdbi user=marco_covielo password=!@5cCgBg3!gb);
	%end;
%mend;
%macro IQ;connection to dbcon %mend;

PROC SQL;
%ConexaoIQ(server=BI);

CREATE TABLE parcelamento AS

SELECT t.*
FROM %IQ
(
SELECT * FROM DWBI_DIME.TbDimContaCartao AS a
INNER join DWBI_DTWH.TbContratoParcelamentoFatura AS b
on a.cddimcontacartao=b.cdcontacartao

WHERE  a.flversaovigente='S'
) AS t
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
%ConexaoIQ(server=BI);
CREATE TABLE contaCPF AS
SELECT t.*
FROM %IQ
(
SELECT
a.CdDimContaCartao,
a.CdDimContaCartaoVersao,
CdPessoaTitularConta, count(*) as qtde FROM DWBI_DIME.TbDimContaCartao AS a
INNER join DWBI_DMOC.TbFatoContaCartao AS b
on a.CdDimContaCartaoVersao=b.CdDimContaCartaoVersao
WHERE a.FlVersaoVigente='S'
group by a.CdDimContaCartao,
a.CdDimContaCartaoVersao,CdPessoaTitularConta
) AS t ;
QUIT;

data wag.parcelamentofatura2;
set parcelamento;
run;

data wag.contaCPF_;
set contaCPF;
run;


data base3040;
set estudos2.CARTAO3040_&Safra_Referencia.; 
format safracontrato ddmmyy10.;
format dtref DT_contrato DT_vencop ddmmyy10.;
dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));
mes1=substr(put(Data_Base,z6.),5,2);
mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);
mes3=substr(DtVencOp,6,2);

ano1=substr(put(Data_Base,z6.),1,4);
ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);
ano3=substr(DtVencOp,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes1||'/'||ano1),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
DT_vencop   = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes3||'/'||ano3),ddmmyy10.);

prazo=          intck('Month',DT_contrato,DT_vencop);
prazo_decorrido=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);
prazo_remane   =intck('Month',dtref,DT_vencop);

contratoparcelado=substr(Contrt,5,40)*1;
mes=substr(DtContr,6,2);
ano=substr(DtContr,1,4);
dia=substr(DtContr,9,2);
safracontrato=input(compress(dia||'/'||mes||'/'||ano),ddmmyy10.);
cdproduto1=cdproduto*1;
TPOP=substr(Contrt,3,2);
if TPOP='04' then DS_OP='Pagamento de Contas'; else
if TPOP='01' then DS_OP='Rotativo'; else
if TPOP='02' then DS_OP='Saque'; else
if TPOP='06' then DS_OP='Fatura Convencional'; else
if TPOP='0A' then DS_OP='Fatura Convencional'; else
if TPOP='0C' then DS_OP='Crediário'; else
if TPOP='05' then DS_OP='Acordo Amigavel'; else
if TPOP='07' then DS_OP='Avista'; else
if TPOP='08' then DS_OP='Limite'; else
if TPOP='0E' then DS_OP='Conta'; else
if TPOP='0B' then DS_OP='CP'; else
if TPOP='03' then DS_OP='Emissor'; else
if TPOP='0D' then DS_OP='Acordo CL'; else
if TPOP='0F' then DS_OP='Fatura Compulsorio';
if DiaAtraso <60 then Over= 'UnderOver60';else Over= 'Over60';
run;


data base3040;
set base3040;
where TPOP in ('03','05','06','0A','0B','0C','0D','0E','0F');
run;

data PARCELAMENTO1;
set wag.parcelamentofatura2;
contratoparcelado=CdContratoSistemaOrigem*1;
run;

proc sort data=PARCELAMENTO1 out=PARCELAMENTO2 nodupkey;
by contratoparcelado;
run;

proc sql;
create table contaCPF_ as (
select
CdDimContaCartao,
CdPessoaTitularConta,
count(*)
from wag.contaCPF_ as a
group by
CdDimContaCartao,
CdPessoaTitularConta);
quit;

data contaCPF_;
set contaCPF_;
nuconta= substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,z11.),3,11)*1;
cpf1=            substr(put(CdPessoaTitularConta,z16.),6,11);
cdproduto=compress(substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,z20.),10,2));
cdproduto1=compress(substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,z20.),10,2))*1;
run;

proc sort data=contaCPF_;
by  cpf1 cdproduto1 descending NUCONTA;
run;

proc sort data=contaCPF_ out=contacpf_1 nodupkey;
by cpf1 cdproduto1;
run;

proc sql;
create table baseparce as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as a
left join PARCELAMENTO2 as b
on a.contratoparcelado=b.contratoparcelado
where tpop in ('0F','06','0A','0C'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table baseCP as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as c
on a.cd = c.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = c.cdproduto
left join PARCELAMENTO2 as b
on a.contratoparcelado=b.contratoparcelado
where tpop in ('0B'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table baseconta as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
contrt,
a.contratoparcelado,
b.contratoparcelado as contraparce,
DtAdesao,
b.CdContaCartao,
DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
a.safracontrato,
TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
CdTipoParcelado,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as c
on a.cd = c.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = c.cdproduto
left join PARCELAMENTO2 as b
on c.CdDimContaCartao=b.CdContaCartao
where tpop in ('03'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table acordoamigavel as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
day(DATA_VENC_1PMT) as DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
c.TAXA as TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VALOR_PMT as VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as b
on a.cd = b.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = b.cdproduto

left join CARTAOBV.TBL_CARTOES_ACORDO as c
on b.NUCONTA        = c.NUCONTA and
   b.cdproduto1     = c.PRODUTO and
   b.cpf1           = c.NUCPFCNPJ and
   a.safracontrato  = c.DATA_EFET
where mod ne '1904' and classop ne 'HH' and tpop in ('05'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table acordoCL_outros as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
compress(substr(DtVencOp,9,2)) as DdVencimentoFatura1,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
. as TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
. as VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as b
on a.cd = b.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = b.cdproduto

where mod ne '1904' and classop ne 'HH' and tpop in ('0D','03','0E'));
quit;

data acordoCL_outros;
set acordoCL_outros;
DdVencimentoFatura=DdVencimentoFatura1*1;
drop DdVencimentoFatura1;
run;

data baseparce_1;
set baseparce baseCP acordoamigavel acordoCL_outros;
if DdVencimentoFatura = . then DdVencimentoFatura=substr(DtVencOp,9,2)*1;
if TxRateAdesao       = . then TxRateAdesao=(((1+(TaxEft/100))**(1/12))-1)*100;
run;

TxRateAdesao,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,

DS_OP,
tipoentrada)

if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr);

if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0270,prazo,VlrContr);

if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0890,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0890,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0890,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0890,prazo,VlrContr);

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr);

if prazo_remane=1 then vl_parcela=saldo;

if TxRateAdesao ne  .                               then novataxa =TxRateAdesao/100  ; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.14498663542780500; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.12587625835053800; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.13839503577447600; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.15523629210019400; else

if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.14210631586999500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.14210631586999500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.13198587630271500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.11968239442808300;

if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05990000000000000; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.05990000000000000; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.05774728633734030; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.05205527972692320;

if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05054148131528880; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.03545880728673000; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.02231777964851410; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.00793117742237048;

if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.0;

if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TxRateAdesao ne . and VrParcelaAdesao=. then vl_parcela=PMT(novataxa,prazo,VlrContr);

format dtref DT_contrato ddmmyy10.;

dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));

mes1='02'; 
mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);

ano1='2025'; 
ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes1||'/'||ano1),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('15'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
prazo_adecorrer=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);

drop dtref DT_contrato ano1 ano2 dia;


run;

data TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT;
set TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT1;
format la1 8.3;
if TipoParcelado='Crédito Pessoal'           then TPOP='0B'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Compulsório'  then TPOP='0F'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Convencional' then TPOP='06'; else
if TipoParcelado='Pgto de Contas Parcelado'  then TPOP='0E'; else
if TipoParcelado='Saque Parcelado'           then TPOP='0A'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo CL'    then TPOP='0D'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo'       then TPOP='05'; else
if TipoParcelado='EMISSOR            '       then TPOP='03';

la1=la;
run;

proc sql;
create table baseparce_11 as (
select
a.*,
case when b.la1 = .  then 0 else  b.la1 end as liquida_ant
from baseparce_1 as a
left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as b
on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazo_decorrido=b.MobContrato and a.prazo=b.prazocontrato
);
quit;

data  baseparce_11;
set baseparce_11;
liquida_ant=0;

run;

%macro sqlloop(start,end);
%DO I=&start. %TO &end.;
   PROC SQL;
       CREATE TABLE BASEPARCE_11 as (
       SELECT b.*,
              case when b.tpop  in ('05','0D')  then 0.1317
                   when a.la1 = . or a.la1 = 0  then 0.01
                   else a.la1 end as LA_&I.
              FROM  BASEPARCE_11 as b
	    left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as a
		on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazocontrato=b.prazo and a.MobContrato= (b.prazo_adecorrer+&I.));
   QUIT;

%END;
%mend;
%sqlloop(1,41);

data BASEPARCE_11;
set BASEPARCE_11;
if tpop  in ('05','0D') then LA_1= 0.05;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_2= 0.05;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_3= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_4= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_5= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_6= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_7= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_8= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_9= 0.065;
if DS_OP='Fatura Convencional' then LA_1= LA_1 * 0.01;
if LA_1 < 0 then LA_1=0;

run;

data baseparce3;
set baseparce_11;

dt='28Feb2025'd;
format dt ddmmyy10.;
diacadocatual =day(dt);
anoatual=year(dt);
mescadocam    = month(dt);
diacontrato= substr(DtContr,9,2)*1;
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam1=1; else mescadocam1   = month(dt)+1;

if mescadocam =11 then mescadocam2=1; else
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam2=2; else mescadocam2   = month(dt)+2;
if DS_OP='Acordo CL' then  novataxa=0;
diacadocam1   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mescadocam1||'/'||anoatual),ddmmyy10.)-1);

prazo_remane1 =prazo_remane+1;
array DiasAtePMT(1:41)      DiasAtePMT_1-DiasAtePMT_41;
array DiasAposPMT(1:41)     DiasAposPMT_1-DiasAposPMT_41;
array SaldoInicioMes(1:41)  SaldoInicioMes_1-SaldoInicioMes_41;
array SaldoAntesPMT(1:41)   SaldoAntesPMT_1-SaldoAntesPMT_41;
array JurosatePMT(1:41)     JurosatePMT_1-JurosatePMT_41;
array VLPMT(1:41)           VLPMT_1-VLPMT_41;
array SaldoAposPMT(1:41)    SaldoAposPMT_1-SaldoAposPMT_41;
array SaldoAposLA(1:41)     SaldoAposLA_1-SaldoAposLA_41;
array SaldoFinalMes(1:41)   SaldoFinalMes_1-SaldoFinalMes_41;
array JurosaposPMT(1:41)    JurosaposPMT_1-JurosaposPMT_41;
array Receita(1:41)         Receita_1-Receita_41;
array meses(1:41)           meses_1-meses_41;
array la(1:41)              la_1-la_41;
j=0;
K=1;
mes=month(dt)+1;
anonovo=year(dt);
do i=1 to 41;
	if prazo_adecorrer + I >= 0 then
		do;
			mes=mes+1;
			if tipoentrada ne 'NV' and k=1 then mes=mes-1;

			if mes =12 then
				do;
					mes=0;
					anonovo=anonovo+1;
				end;
      		diacadocam2   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mes+1||'/'||anonovo),ddmmyy10.)-1);
      		meses(i)     =diacadocam2;
      		DiasAtePMT(I)=DdVencimentoFatura;
			if K=1 then
				do;
					k=2;
            		prazo_remane22=prazo_remane+I-1;
					prazo_remane11=prazo_remane1+I-1;
					if tipoentrada='NV' then
		 				do;
						   
						    DiasAposPMT(I)=30-diacontrato;
	    					SaldoInicioMes(I)=VlrContr;
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =VlrContr;
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I)*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I);
						end;
					else
						do;
							DiasAtePMT(I)    =DdVencimentoFatura;
							

							DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
							SaldoInicioMes(I)=Saldo;
							SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
							JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
							if prazo_remane = 1 then
								VLPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I);
							else
								VLPMT(I) = PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoInicioMes(I));
							if tipoentrada= 'NV' and prazo_remane1 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)  =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);

							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
						end;
				end;
			else
				do;
					
					DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
					SaldoInicioMes(I)=SaldoFinalMes(I-1);
					SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
					JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
					if tipoentrada='NV'  then
						do;
							VLPMT(I) =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane1-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane11 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;
					else
						do;
							VLPMT(I)     =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane22 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;

					SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
					JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
					if tpop = '05' then
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
					else
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
				end;
			j=j+1;
		end;
end;
run;


data BASEPARCE3;
set BASEPARCE3;
safracontrato2=substr(DtContr,1,7);
run;

		  (SUM(t1.Receita_32)) AS SUM_of_Receita_32,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_33)) AS SUM_of_Receita_33,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_34)) AS SUM_of_Receita_34,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_35)) AS SUM_of_Receita_35,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_36)) AS SUM_of_Receita_36,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_37)) AS SUM_of_Receita_37,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_38)) AS SUM_of_Receita_38,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_39)) AS SUM_of_Receita_39,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_40)) AS SUM_of_Receita_40,
		  (SUM(t1.Receita_41)) AS SUM_of_Receita_41,

		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_1)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_1,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_2)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_2,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_3)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_3,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_4)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_4,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_5)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_5,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_6)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_6,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_7)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_7,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_8)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_8,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_9)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_9,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_10)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_10,

          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_12)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_12,

          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_15)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_15,

          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_18)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_18,

		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_23)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_23,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_24)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_24,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_25)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_25,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_26)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_26,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_27)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_27,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_28)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_28,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_29)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_29,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_30)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_30,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_31)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_31,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_32)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_32,

		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_34)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_34,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_35)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_35,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_36)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_36,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_37)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_37,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_38)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_38,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_39)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_39,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_40)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_40,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_41)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_41

      FROM WORK.BASEPARCE3 t1
      GROUP BY t1.Data_Base,
               t1.tipoentrada,
               t1.Mod,
               t1.TPOP,
               t1.DS_OP;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	SELECT COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Min_Saldo,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Max_Saldo,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_Receita,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_Receita,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_PMT,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_PMT

		INTO: Min_Saldo,
		    : Max_Saldo,
			: Min_Receita,
		    : Max_Receita,
			: Min_PMT,
		    : Max_PMT

	FROM dictionary.columns
	WHERE libname= upcase("WORK") AND memname = upcase("QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31") AND name LIKE "SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_%" escape '#';
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
SELECT DISTINCT MAX(INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e')) AS DataRef
	INTO: Data_Ref
FROM BASE3040;
QUIT;

DATA TBL_APOIO_1;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Saldo.--&Max_Saldo.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_2;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Receita.--&Max_Receita.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_3;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_PMT.--&Max_PMT.);
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_1 OUT=TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_2 OUT=TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_Receita_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_3 OUT=TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_VLPMT_:;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.) AS Mob_Contrato,
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE

	FROM TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_Receita_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_Receita_',''),BEST12.) AS Mob_Contrato,
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Receita Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Receita Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Receita Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_VLPMT_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_VLPMT_',''),BEST12.) AS Mob_Contrato,
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'PMT Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'PMT Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'PMT Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_OUTPUT_MODELOSAS AS

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)*(b.Qtd_DiasContabeis/30)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP_1 AS a
	LEFT JOIN CARTAOBV.TBL_DIASCONTABEIS_CARTOES AS b
	ON a.SafraRef = b.MovRef
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0
;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0890,prazo,VlrContr);

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr); else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr);

if prazo_remane=1 then vl_parcela=saldo;

if TxRateAdesao ne  .                               then novataxa =TxRateAdesao/100  ; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.14498663542780500; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.12587625835053800; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.13839503577447600; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.15523629210019400; else

if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.14210631586999500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.14210631586999500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.13198587630271500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.11968239442808300;

if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05990000000000000; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.05990000000000000; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.05774728633734030; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.05205527972692320;

if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05054148131528880; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.03545880728673000; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.02231777964851410; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.00793117742237048;

if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.0;

if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TxRateAdesao ne . then vl_parcela=PMT(novataxa,prazo,VlrContr);

format dtref DT_contrato ddmmyy10.;

dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));

mes1='01'; 
mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);

ano1='2025'; 
ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes1||'/'||ano1),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('15'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
prazo_adecorrer=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);

drop dtref DT_contrato ano1 ano2 dia;

run;

data TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT;
set TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT1;
format la1 8.3;
if TipoParcelado='Crédito Pessoal'           then TPOP='0B'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Compulsório'  then TPOP='0F'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Convencional' then TPOP='06'; else
if TipoParcelado='Pgto de Contas Parcelado'  then TPOP='0E'; else
if TipoParcelado='Saque Parcelado'           then TPOP='0A'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo CL'    then TPOP='0D'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo'       then TPOP='05'; else
if TipoParcelado='EMISSOR            '       then TPOP='03';

la1=la;
run;

proc sql;
create table BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11 as (
select
a.*,
case when b.la1 = .  then 0 else  b.la1 end as liquida_ant
from BASEPARCE_NOVOS_1 as a
left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as b
on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazo_decorrido=b.MobContrato and a.prazo=b.prazocontrato
);
quit;

DATA BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
	SET BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
	liquida_ant=0;
RUN;

%macro sqlloop(start,end);
%DO I=&start. %TO &end.;
   PROC SQL;
       CREATE TABLE BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11 as (
       SELECT b.*,
              case when b.tpop  in ('05','0D')  then 0.1317
                   when a.la1 = . or a.la1 = 0  then 0.01
                   else a.la1 end as LA_&I.
              FROM  BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11 as b
	    left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as a
		on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazocontrato=b.prazo and a.MobContrato= (b.prazo_adecorrer+&I.));
   QUIT;

%END;
%mend;
%sqlloop(1,41);

DATA BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
SET BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
	if tpop  in ('05','0D') then LA_1= 0.05;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_2= 0.05;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_3= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_4= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_5= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_6= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_7= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_8= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_9= 0.065;
	if DS_OP='Fatura Convencional' then LA_1= LA_1 * 0.01;
	if LA_1 < 0 then LA_1=0;
RUN;

data BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
set BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;

dt='31Jan2025'd;
format dt ddmmyy10.;
diacadocatual =day(dt);
anoatual=year(dt);
mescadocam    = month(dt);
diacontrato= substr(DtContr,9,2)*1;
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam1=1; else mescadocam1   = month(dt)+1;

if mescadocam =11 then mescadocam2=1; else
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam2=2; else mescadocam2   = month(dt)+2;
if DS_OP='Acordo CL' then  novataxa=0;
diacadocam1   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mescadocam1||'/'||anoatual),ddmmyy10.)-1);

prazo_remane1 =prazo_remane+1;
array DiasAtePMT(1:41)      DiasAtePMT_1-DiasAtePMT_41;
array DiasAposPMT(1:41)     DiasAposPMT_1-DiasAposPMT_41;
array SaldoInicioMes(1:41)  SaldoInicioMes_1-SaldoInicioMes_41;
array SaldoAntesPMT(1:41)   SaldoAntesPMT_1-SaldoAntesPMT_41;
array JurosatePMT(1:41)     JurosatePMT_1-JurosatePMT_41;
array VLPMT(1:41)           VLPMT_1-VLPMT_41;
array SaldoAposPMT(1:41)    SaldoAposPMT_1-SaldoAposPMT_41;
array SaldoAposLA(1:41)     SaldoAposLA_1-SaldoAposLA_41;
array SaldoFinalMes(1:41)   SaldoFinalMes_1-SaldoFinalMes_41;
array JurosaposPMT(1:41)    JurosaposPMT_1-JurosaposPMT_41;
array Receita(1:41)         Receita_1-Receita_41;
array meses(1:41)           meses_1-meses_41;
array la(1:41)              la_1-la_41;
j=0;
K=1;
mes=month(dt)+1;
anonovo=year(dt);
do i=1 to 41;
	if prazo_adecorrer + I >= 0 then
		do;
			mes=mes+1;
			if tipoentrada ne 'NV' and k=1 then mes=mes-1;

			if mes =12 then
				do;
					mes=0;
					anonovo=anonovo+1;
				end;
      		diacadocam2   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mes+1||'/'||anonovo),ddmmyy10.)-1);
      		meses(i)     =diacadocam2;
      		DiasAtePMT(I)=DdVencimentoFatura;
			if K=1 then
				do;
					k=2;
            		prazo_remane22=prazo_remane+I-1;
					prazo_remane11=prazo_remane1+I-1;
					if tipoentrada='NV' then
		 				do;
						   
						    DiasAposPMT(I)=30-diacontrato;
	    					SaldoInicioMes(I)=VlrContr;
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =VlrContr;
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I)*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I);
						end;
					else
						do;
							DiasAtePMT(I)    =DdVencimentoFatura;

							DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
							SaldoInicioMes(I)=Saldo;
							SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
							JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
							if prazo_remane = 1 then
								VLPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I);
							else
								VLPMT(I) = PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoInicioMes(I));
							if tipoentrada= 'NV' and prazo_remane1 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)  =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);

							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
						end;
				end;
			else
				do;
					DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
					SaldoInicioMes(I)=SaldoFinalMes(I-1);
					SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
					JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
					if tipoentrada='NV'  then
						do;
							VLPMT(I) =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane1-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane11 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;
					else
						do;
							VLPMT(I)     =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane22 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;

					SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
					JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
					if tpop = '05' then
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
					else
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
				end;
			j=j+1;
		end;
end;
run;

data BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
set BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
safracontrato2=substr(DtContr,1,7);
run;

          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_8)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_8,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_9)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_9,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_10)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_10,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_11)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_11,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_12)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_12,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_13)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_13,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_14)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_14,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_15)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_15,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_16)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_16,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_17)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_17,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_18)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_18,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_19)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_19,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_20)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_20,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_21)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_21,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_22)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_22,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_23)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_23,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_24)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_24,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_25)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_25,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_26)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_26,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_27)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_27,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_28)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_28,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_29)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_29,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_30)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_30,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_31)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_31,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_32)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_32,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_33)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_33,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_34)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_34,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_35)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_35,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_36)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_36,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_37)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_37,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_38)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_38,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_39)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_39,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_40)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_40,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_41)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_41

      FROM WORK.BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3 t1
      GROUP BY t1.Data_Base,
               t1.tipoentrada,
               t1.Mod,
               t1.TPOP,
               t1.DS_OP;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	SELECT COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Min_Saldo,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Max_Saldo,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_Receita,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_Receita,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_PMT,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_PMT

		INTO: Min_Saldo,
		    : Max_Saldo,
			: Min_Receita,
		    : Max_Receita,
			: Min_PMT,
		    : Max_PMT

	FROM dictionary.columns
	WHERE libname= upcase("WORK") AND memname = upcase("QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31") AND name LIKE "SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_%" escape '#';
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
SELECT DISTINCT MIN(INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0)) AS DataRef
	INTO: Data_Ref
FROM BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
QUIT;

DATA TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Saldo.--&Max_Saldo.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Receita.--&Max_Receita.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_PMT.--&Max_PMT.);
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1 OUT=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2 OUT=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_Receita_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3 OUT=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_VLPMT_:;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.) as Adicional,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   &Data_Ref. as Data format date9.,
		   
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,
		   INTCK('MONTH',CALCULATED DataCtt,CALCULATED DataRef) AS Mob_Contrato,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE

	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_Receita_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   &Data_Ref. as Data format date9.,
		   
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,
		   INTCK('MONTH',CALCULATED DataCtt,CALCULATED DataRef) AS Mob_Contrato,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Receita Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Receita Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Receita Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_VLPMT_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   &Data_Ref. as Data format date9.,
		   
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,
		   INTCK('MONTH',CALCULATED DataCtt,CALCULATED DataRef) AS Mob_Contrato,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'PMT Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'PMT Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'PMT Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_OUTPUT_PISMO_MODELOSAS AS

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)*(b.Qtd_DiasContabeis/30)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP_1 AS a
	LEFT JOIN CARTAOBV.TBL_DIASCONTABEIS_CARTOES AS b
	ON a.SafraRef = b.MovRef
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0
;
QUIT;

OPTIONS COMPRESS=YES;
LIBNAME CARTAO1    '/riscosbv/cartoes/PORTFOLIO';
LIBNAME CARTAO '/riscosbvf/financas/varejo/Cartoes';
LIBNAME Erick2 '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/edmiyasa';
LIBNAME bhu '/projetos/ctrcredito/modelos/historico/bhrodas';
LIBNAME MIS2 '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/fatigatte/MIS';
LIBNAME Dan '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/dannakagawa';
LIBNAME estudos2 '/riscosbvf/indicadores/Estudos_2';
LIBNAME CARNEIRO '/riscosbv/cobranca/jcarneiro/';
LIBNAME PG_CYBER	BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/jcarneiro/pagos_cyber';
LIBNAME WAG '/riscosbvf/pol_empcr/wsilva';

%global mes1 ano1 Data_Referencia dt Safra_Referencia;

%LET Data_Referencia = '01Jan2026'd; 
%LET dt = '01Jan2026'd; 
%LET Safra_Referencia = 202601; 

%LET mes1='01'; 
%LET ano1='2026'; 

%PUT &Data_Referencia.;
%PUT &Safra_Referencia.;
%PUT &mes1.;
%PUT &ano1.;


%global basecpf baseParcelamento baseParcelamentoNoDup;

%LET basecpf = CONTACPF_BN; 
%LET baseParcelamento = PARCELAMENTOFATURA_BN; 
%LET baseParcelamentoNoDup = PARCELAMENTO_BN; 

%PUT &Data_Referencia. &baseParcelamento. &baseParcelamentoNoDup.;

PROC SQL ;
CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM (project='bv-safn-dtm' credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/fn/safn-dtm.json' schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage);
	CREATE TABLE parcelamento AS
	SELECT * FROM CONNECTION TO PERM
    (
	SELECT * FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dwbi_dime.tbdimcontacartao  AS a
	INNER join bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dwbi_dtwh.tbcontratoparcelamentofatura  AS b
	on a.cddimcontacartao=b.cdcontacartao

	WHERE  a.flversaovigente='S'
	) AS t;
DISCONNECT FROM PERM;
QUIT;

PROC SQL ;
CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM (project='bv-safn-dtm' credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/fn/safn-dtm.json' schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage);
	CREATE TABLE contaCPF AS
	SELECT t.CdDimContaCartao,
		   t.CdDimContaCartaoVersao FORMAT COMMAX18.,
		   t.CdPessoaTitularConta FORMAT COMMAX18.,
	       t.Qtde
    FROM CONNECTION TO PERM
    (
		SELECT CAST(a.CdDimContaCartao AS NUMERIC) AS CdDimContaCartao,
			   CAST(a.CdDimContaCartaoVersao AS NUMERIC) AS CdDimContaCartaoVersao,
			   CAST(CdPessoaTitularConta AS NUMERIC) AS CdPessoaTitularConta,
	           COUNT(*) as Qtde
	    FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dwbi_dime.tbdimcontacartao AS a
		INNER join bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dwbi_dmoc.tbfatocontacartao AS b
		ON a.CdDimContaCartaoVersao=b.CdDimContaCartaoVersao
		WHERE a.FlVersaoVigente='S'
		GROUP BY 1,2,3
	) AS t;
	DISCONNECT FROM PERM;
QUIT;

data wag.&baseParcelamento.;
set parcelamento;
run;

data wag.&basecpf.;
set contaCPF;
run;

data PARCELAMENTO1 (KEEP= DtAdesao CdContaCartao DdVencimentoFatura TxRateAdesao VrParcelaAdesao CdTipoParcelado CdContratoSistemaOrigem contratoparcelado);
set wag.&baseParcelamento.;
contratoparcelado=prxchange('s/[A-Za-z]//', -1, CdContratoSistemaOrigem)*1;


if vtype(CdContaCartao) = 'C' then do;
temp = compress(CdContaCartao, , 'kd'); 
if not missing(temp) then CdContaCartao2 = input(temp, best32.);
end;
else CdContaCartao2 = CdContaCartao;


if vtype(ddvencimentofatura) = 'C' then do;
   if not missing(ddvencimentofatura) then ddvencimentofatura2 = input(ddvencimentofatura, best32.);
end;
else ddvencimentofatura2 = ddvencimentofatura;


if vtype(txrateadesao) = 'C' then do;
   if not missing(txrateadesao) then txrateadesao2 = input(txrateadesao, best32.);
end;
else txrateadesao2 = txrateadesao;


if vtype(vrparcelaadesao) = 'C' then do;
   if not missing(vrparcelaadesao) then vrparcelaadesao2 = input(vrparcelaadesao, best32.);
end;
else vrparcelaadesao2 = vrparcelaadesao;

drop temp;
drop CdContaCartao ddvencimentofatura txrateadesao vrparcelaadesao;

rename CdContaCartao2 = CdContaCartao;
rename ddvencimentofatura2 = ddvencimentofatura;
rename txrateadesao2 = txrateadesao;
rename vrparcelaadesao2 = vrparcelaadesao;

run;

proc sort data=PARCELAMENTO1 out=&baseParcelamentoNoDup. nodupkey;
by contratoparcelado;
run;

DATA WAG.&baseParcelamentoNoDup. (compress=yes);
SET WORK.&baseParcelamentoNoDup.;
RUN;



proc sql;
create table contaCPF_ as (
select
CdDimContaCartao,
CdPessoaTitularConta,
count(*)
from wag.&basecpf. as a
group by
CdDimContaCartao,
CdPessoaTitularConta);
quit;

data contaCPF_;
set contaCPF_;
nuconta= substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,z11.),3,11)*1;
cpf1= substr(put(CdPessoaTitularConta,z16.),6,11);
cdproduto=compress(substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,z20.),10,2));
cdproduto1=compress(substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,z20.),10,2))*1;
run;

proc sort data=contaCPF_;
by  cpf1 cdproduto1 descending NUCONTA;
run;

proc sort data=contaCPF_ out=contacpf_1 nodupkey;
by cpf1 cdproduto1;
run;


data base3040;
set estudos2.CARTAO3040_&Safra_Referencia.; 
format safracontrato ddmmyy10.;
format dtref DT_contrato DT_vencop ddmmyy10.;
dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));
mes1=substr(put(Data_Base,z6.),5,2);
mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);
mes3=substr(DtVencOp,6,2);

ano1=substr(put(Data_Base,z6.),1,4);
ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);
ano3=substr(DtVencOp,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes1||'/'||ano1),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
DT_vencop   = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes3||'/'||ano3),ddmmyy10.);

prazo=          intck('Month',DT_contrato,DT_vencop);
prazo_decorrido=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);
prazo_remane   =intck('Month',dtref,DT_vencop);

contratoparcelado=substr(Contrt,5,40)*1;
mes=substr(DtContr,6,2);
ano=substr(DtContr,1,4);
dia=substr(DtContr,9,2);
safracontrato=input(compress(dia||'/'||mes||'/'||ano),ddmmyy10.);
cdproduto1=cdproduto*1;
TPOP=substr(Contrt,3,2);
if TPOP='04' then DS_OP='Pagamento de Contas'; else
if TPOP='01' then DS_OP='Rotativo'; else
if TPOP='02' then DS_OP='Saque'; else
if TPOP='06' then DS_OP='Fatura Convencional'; else
if TPOP='0A' then DS_OP='Fatura Convencional'; else
if TPOP='0C' then DS_OP='Crediário'; else
if TPOP='05' then DS_OP='Acordo Amigavel'; else
if TPOP='07' then DS_OP='Avista'; else
if TPOP='08' then DS_OP='Limite'; else
if TPOP='0E' then DS_OP='Conta'; else
if TPOP='0B' then DS_OP='CP'; else
if TPOP='03' then DS_OP='Emissor'; else
if TPOP='0D' then DS_OP='Acordo CL'; else
if TPOP='0F' then DS_OP='Fatura Compulsorio';
if DiaAtraso <60 then Over= 'UnderOver60';else Over= 'Over60';
run;


data base3040;
set base3040;
where TPOP in ('03','05','06','0A','0B','0C','0D','0E','0F');
run;

proc sql;
create table baseparce as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as a
left join wag.&baseParcelamentoNoDup. as b
on a.contratoparcelado=b.contratoparcelado
where tpop in ('0F','06','0A','0C'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table baseCP as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as c
on a.cd = c.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = c.cdproduto
left join wag.&baseParcelamentoNoDup. as b
on a.contratoparcelado=b.contratoparcelado
where tpop in ('0B'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table baseconta as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
contrt,
a.contratoparcelado,
b.contratoparcelado as contraparce,
DtAdesao,
b.CdContaCartao,
DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
a.safracontrato,
TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
CdTipoParcelado,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as c
on a.cd = c.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = c.cdproduto
left join wag.&baseParcelamentoNoDup. as b
on c.CdDimContaCartao=b.CdContaCartao
where tpop in ('03'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table acordoamigavel as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
day(DATA_VENC_1PMT) as DdVencimentoFatura,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
c.TAXA as TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
VALOR_PMT as VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as b
on a.cd = b.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = b.cdproduto

left join CARTAOBV.TBL_CARTOES_ACORDO as c
on b.NUCONTA        = c.NUCONTA and
   b.cdproduto1     = c.PRODUTO and
   b.cpf1           = c.NUCPFCNPJ and
   a.safracontrato  = c.DATA_EFET
where mod ne '1904' and classop ne 'HH' and tpop in ('05'));
quit;

proc sql;
create table acordoCL_outros as (
select
cd,
mod,
prazo,
prazo_decorrido,
VlrContr,
a.contratoparcelado,
compress(substr(DtVencOp,9,2)) as DdVencimentoFatura1,
Data_Base,
a.prazo_remane,
DtContr,
. as TxRateAdesao,
TaxEft,
saldo,
. as VrParcelaAdesao,
DtVencOp,
TPOP,
DS_OP,
'BAU' as tipoentrada
from base3040 as  a
left join contacpf_1 as b
on a.cd = b.cpf1 and a.cdproduto = b.cdproduto

where mod ne '1904' and classop ne 'HH' and tpop in ('0D','03','0E'));
quit;

data acordoCL_outros;
set acordoCL_outros;
DdVencimentoFatura=DdVencimentoFatura1*1;
drop DdVencimentoFatura1;
run;

data baseparce_1;
set baseparce baseCP acordoamigavel acordoCL_outros;
if DdVencimentoFatura = . then DdVencimentoFatura=substr(DtVencOp,9,2)*1;
if TxRateAdesao       = . then TxRateAdesao=(((1+(TaxEft/100))**(1/12))-1)*100;
run;

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then vl_parcela = PMT(0.0280,prazo,VlrContr);

if prazo_remane=1 then vl_parcela=saldo;

if TxRateAdesao ne  .                               then novataxa =TxRateAdesao/100  ; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.14498663542780500; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.12587625835053800; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.13839503577447600; else
if TPOP='0F' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.15523629210019400; else

if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.14210631586999500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.14210631586999500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.13198587630271500; else
if TPOP='06' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.11968239442808300;

if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05990000000000000; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.05990000000000000; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.05774728633734030; else
if TPOP='0B' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.05205527972692320;

if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05054148131528880; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.03545880728673000; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.02231777964851410; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.00793117742237048;

if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.0;

if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TxRateAdesao ne . and VrParcelaAdesao=. then vl_parcela=PMT(novataxa,prazo,VlrContr);

format dtref DT_contrato ddmmyy10.;

dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));


mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);


ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||&mes1.||'/'||&ano1.),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('15'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
prazo_adecorrer=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);

drop dtref DT_contrato ano1 ano2 dia;


run;

data TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT;
set TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT1;
format la1 8.3;
if TipoParcelado='Crédito Pessoal'           then TPOP='0B'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Compulsório'  then TPOP='0F'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Convencional' then TPOP='06'; else
if TipoParcelado='Pgto de Contas Parcelado'  then TPOP='0E'; else
if TipoParcelado='Saque Parcelado'           then TPOP='0A'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo CL'    then TPOP='0D'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo'       then TPOP='05'; else
if TipoParcelado='EMISSOR            '       then TPOP='03';

la1=la;
run;

proc sql;
create table baseparce_11 as (
select
a.*,
case when b.la1 = .  then 0 else  b.la1 end as liquida_ant
from baseparce_1 as a
left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as b
on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazo_decorrido=b.MobContrato and a.prazo=b.prazocontrato
);
quit;

data  baseparce_11;
set baseparce_11;
liquida_ant=0;

run;

%macro sqlloop(start,end);
%DO I=&start. %TO &end.;
   PROC SQL;
       CREATE TABLE BASEPARCE_11 as (
       SELECT b.*,
              case when b.tpop  in ('05','0D')  then 0.1317
                   when a.la1 = . or a.la1 = 0  then 0.01
                   else a.la1 end as LA_&I.
              FROM  BASEPARCE_11 as b
	    left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as a
		on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazocontrato=b.prazo and a.MobContrato= (b.prazo_adecorrer+&I.));
   QUIT;

%END;
%mend;
%sqlloop(1,41);

data BASEPARCE_11;
set BASEPARCE_11;
if tpop  in ('05','0D') then LA_1= 0.05;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_2= 0.05;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_3= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_4= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_5= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_6= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_7= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_8= 0.065;
if tpop  in ('05') then LA_9= 0.065;
if DS_OP='Fatura Convencional' then LA_1= LA_1 * 0.01;
if LA_1 < 0 then LA_1=0;

run;

data baseparce3;
set baseparce_11;

dt=&dt.;
format dt ddmmyy10.;
diacadocatual =day(dt);
anoatual=year(dt);
mescadocam    = month(dt);
diacontrato= substr(DtContr,9,2)*1;
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam1=1; else mescadocam1   = month(dt)+1;

if mescadocam =11 then mescadocam2=1; else
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam2=2; else mescadocam2   = month(dt)+2;
if DS_OP='Acordo CL' then  novataxa=0;
diacadocam1   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mescadocam1||'/'||anoatual),ddmmyy10.)-1);

prazo_remane1 =prazo_remane+1;
array DiasAtePMT(1:41)      DiasAtePMT_1-DiasAtePMT_41;
array DiasAposPMT(1:41)     DiasAposPMT_1-DiasAposPMT_41;
array SaldoInicioMes(1:41)  SaldoInicioMes_1-SaldoInicioMes_41;
array SaldoAntesPMT(1:41)   SaldoAntesPMT_1-SaldoAntesPMT_41;
array JurosatePMT(1:41)     JurosatePMT_1-JurosatePMT_41;
array VLPMT(1:41)           VLPMT_1-VLPMT_41;
array SaldoAposPMT(1:41)    SaldoAposPMT_1-SaldoAposPMT_41;
array SaldoAposLA(1:41)     SaldoAposLA_1-SaldoAposLA_41;
array SaldoFinalMes(1:41)   SaldoFinalMes_1-SaldoFinalMes_41;
array JurosaposPMT(1:41)    JurosaposPMT_1-JurosaposPMT_41;
array Receita(1:41)         Receita_1-Receita_41;
array meses(1:41)           meses_1-meses_41;
array la(1:41)              la_1-la_41;
j=0;
K=1;
mes=month(dt)+1;
anonovo=year(dt);
do i=1 to 41;
	if prazo_adecorrer + I >= 0 then
		do;
			mes=mes+1;
			if tipoentrada ne 'NV' and k=1 then mes=mes-1;

			if mes =12 then
				do;
					mes=0;
					anonovo=anonovo+1;
				end;
      		diacadocam2   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mes+1||'/'||anonovo),ddmmyy10.)-1);
      		meses(i)     =diacadocam2;
      		DiasAtePMT(I)=DdVencimentoFatura;
			if K=1 then
				do;
					k=2;
            		prazo_remane22=prazo_remane+I-1;
					prazo_remane11=prazo_remane1+I-1;
					if tipoentrada='NV' then
		 				do;
						   
						    DiasAposPMT(I)=30-diacontrato;
	    					SaldoInicioMes(I)=VlrContr;
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =VlrContr;
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I)*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I);
						end;
					else
						do;
							DiasAtePMT(I)    =DdVencimentoFatura;
							

							DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
							SaldoInicioMes(I)=Saldo;
							SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
							JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
							if prazo_remane = 1 then
								VLPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I);
							else
								VLPMT(I) = PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoInicioMes(I));
							if tipoentrada= 'NV' and prazo_remane1 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)  =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);

							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
						end;
				end;
			else
				do;
					
					DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
					SaldoInicioMes(I)=SaldoFinalMes(I-1);
					SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
					JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
					if tipoentrada='NV'  then
						do;
							VLPMT(I) =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane1-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane11 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;
					else
						do;
							VLPMT(I)     =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane22 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;

					SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
					JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
					if tpop = '05' then
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
					else
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
				end;
			j=j+1;
		end;
end;
run;


data BASEPARCE3;
set BASEPARCE3;
safracontrato2=substr(DtContr,1,7);
run;

          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_19)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_19,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_20)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_20,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_21)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_21,
          (SUM(t1.VLPMT_22)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_22,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_23)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_23,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_24)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_24,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_25)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_25,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_26)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_26,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_27)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_27,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_28)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_28,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_29)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_29,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_30)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_30,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_31)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_31,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_32)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_32,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_33)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_33,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_34)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_34,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_35)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_35,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_36)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_36,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_37)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_37,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_38)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_38,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_39)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_39,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_40)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_40,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_41)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_41

      FROM WORK.BASEPARCE3 t1
      GROUP BY t1.Data_Base,
               t1.tipoentrada,
               t1.Mod,
               t1.TPOP,
               t1.DS_OP;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	SELECT COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Min_Saldo,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Max_Saldo,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_Receita,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_Receita,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_PMT,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_PMT

		INTO: Min_Saldo,
		    : Max_Saldo,
			: Min_Receita,
		    : Max_Receita,
			: Min_PMT,
		    : Max_PMT

	FROM dictionary.columns
	WHERE libname= upcase("WORK") AND memname = upcase("QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31") AND name LIKE "SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_%" escape '#';
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
SELECT DISTINCT MAX(INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e')) AS DataRef
	INTO: Data_Ref
FROM BASE3040;
QUIT;

DATA TBL_APOIO_1;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Saldo.--&Max_Saldo.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_2;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Receita.--&Max_Receita.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_3;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_PMT.--&Max_PMT.);
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_1 OUT=TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_2 OUT=TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_Receita_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_3 OUT=TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_VLPMT_:;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.) AS Mob_Contrato,
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE

	FROM TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_Receita_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_Receita_',''),BEST12.) AS Mob_Contrato,
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Receita Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Receita Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Receita Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_VLPMT_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_VLPMT_',''),BEST12.) AS Mob_Contrato,
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'PMT Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'PMT Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'PMT Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_OUTPUT_MODELOSAS AS

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_1_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)*(b.Qtd_DiasContabeis/30)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_2_TRANSP_1 AS a
	LEFT JOIN CARTAOBV.TBL_DIASCONTABEIS_CARTOES AS b
	ON a.SafraRef = b.MovRef
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_3_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0
;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: 00- Extração Output Padrão (Carteira Legado) GCP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 007 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
