if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.05054148131528880; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.03545880728673000; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.02231777964851410; else
if TPOP='05' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.00793117742237048;

if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6    then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18   then novataxa =0.0; else
if TPOP='0D' and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18   then novataxa =0.0;

if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0C') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0A') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('03') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 6  then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 12 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo <= 18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000; else
if TPOP=('0E') and TxRateAdesao = . and prazo >  18 then novataxa =0.02700000000000000;

if TxRateAdesao ne . then vl_parcela=PMT(novataxa,prazo,VlrContr);

format dtref DT_contrato ddmmyy10.;

dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));

mes1='01'; 
mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);

ano1='2025'; 
ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes1||'/'||ano1),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('15'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
prazo_adecorrer=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);

drop dtref DT_contrato ano1 ano2 dia;

run;

data TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT;
set TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT1;
format la1 8.3;
if TipoParcelado='Crédito Pessoal'           then TPOP='0B'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Compulsório'  then TPOP='0F'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Convencional' then TPOP='06'; else
if TipoParcelado='Pgto de Contas Parcelado'  then TPOP='0E'; else
if TipoParcelado='Saque Parcelado'           then TPOP='0A'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo CL'    then TPOP='0D'; else
if TipoParcelado='Parcelamento Acordo'       then TPOP='05'; else
if TipoParcelado='EMISSOR            '       then TPOP='03';

la1=la;
run;

proc sql;
create table BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11 as (
select
a.*,
case when b.la1 = .  then 0 else  b.la1 end as liquida_ant
from BASEPARCE_NOVOS_1 as a
left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as b
on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazo_decorrido=b.MobContrato and a.prazo=b.prazocontrato
);
quit;

DATA BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
	SET BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
	liquida_ant=0;
RUN;

%macro sqlloop(start,end);
%DO I=&start. %TO &end.;
   PROC SQL;
       CREATE TABLE BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11 as (
       SELECT b.*,
              case when b.tpop  in ('05','0D')  then 0.1317
                   when a.la1 = . or a.la1 = 0  then 0.01
                   else a.la1 end as LA_&I.
              FROM  BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11 as b
	    left join TBL_CARTOES_LA_IMPORT as a
		on a.tpop=b.tpop and a.prazocontrato=b.prazo and a.MobContrato= (b.prazo_adecorrer+&I.));
   QUIT;

%END;
%mend;
%sqlloop(1,41);

DATA BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
SET BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;
	if tpop  in ('05','0D') then LA_1= 0.05;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_2= 0.05;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_3= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_4= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_5= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_6= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_7= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_8= 0.065;
	if tpop  in ('05') then LA_9= 0.065;
	if DS_OP='Fatura Convencional' then LA_1= LA_1 * 0.01;
	if LA_1 < 0 then LA_1=0;
RUN;

data BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
set BASEPARCE_NOVOS_11;

dt='31Jan2025'd;
format dt ddmmyy10.;
diacadocatual =day(dt);
anoatual=year(dt);
mescadocam    = month(dt);
diacontrato= substr(DtContr,9,2)*1;
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam1=1; else mescadocam1   = month(dt)+1;

if mescadocam =11 then mescadocam2=1; else
if mescadocam =12 then mescadocam2=2; else mescadocam2   = month(dt)+2;
if DS_OP='Acordo CL' then  novataxa=0;
diacadocam1   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mescadocam1||'/'||anoatual),ddmmyy10.)-1);

prazo_remane1 =prazo_remane+1;
array DiasAtePMT(1:41)      DiasAtePMT_1-DiasAtePMT_41;
array DiasAposPMT(1:41)     DiasAposPMT_1-DiasAposPMT_41;
array SaldoInicioMes(1:41)  SaldoInicioMes_1-SaldoInicioMes_41;
array SaldoAntesPMT(1:41)   SaldoAntesPMT_1-SaldoAntesPMT_41;
array JurosatePMT(1:41)     JurosatePMT_1-JurosatePMT_41;
array VLPMT(1:41)           VLPMT_1-VLPMT_41;
array SaldoAposPMT(1:41)    SaldoAposPMT_1-SaldoAposPMT_41;
array SaldoAposLA(1:41)     SaldoAposLA_1-SaldoAposLA_41;
array SaldoFinalMes(1:41)   SaldoFinalMes_1-SaldoFinalMes_41;
array JurosaposPMT(1:41)    JurosaposPMT_1-JurosaposPMT_41;
array Receita(1:41)         Receita_1-Receita_41;
array meses(1:41)           meses_1-meses_41;
array la(1:41)              la_1-la_41;
j=0;
K=1;
mes=month(dt)+1;
anonovo=year(dt);
do i=1 to 41;
	if prazo_adecorrer + I >= 0 then
		do;
			mes=mes+1;
			if tipoentrada ne 'NV' and k=1 then mes=mes-1;

			if mes =12 then
				do;
					mes=0;
					anonovo=anonovo+1;
				end;
      		diacadocam2   = day(input(compress('01'||'/'||mes+1||'/'||anonovo),ddmmyy10.)-1);
      		meses(i)     =diacadocam2;
      		DiasAtePMT(I)=DdVencimentoFatura;
			if K=1 then
				do;
					k=2;
            		prazo_remane22=prazo_remane+I-1;
					prazo_remane11=prazo_remane1+I-1;
					if tipoentrada='NV' then
		 				do;
						   
						    DiasAposPMT(I)=30-diacontrato;
	    					SaldoInicioMes(I)=VlrContr;
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =VlrContr;
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I)*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =JurosaposPMT(I);
						end;
					else
						do;
							DiasAtePMT(I)    =DdVencimentoFatura;

							DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
							SaldoInicioMes(I)=Saldo;
							SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
							JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
							if prazo_remane = 1 then
								VLPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I);
							else
								VLPMT(I) = PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoInicioMes(I));
							if tipoentrada= 'NV' and prazo_remane1 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)  =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
							SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
							JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);

							if tpop = '05' then
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
							else
								Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
						end;
				end;
			else
				do;
					DiasAposPMT(I)   =30-DdVencimentoFatura;
					SaldoInicioMes(I)=SaldoFinalMes(I-1);
					SaldoAntesPMT(I) =SaldoInicioMes(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAtePMT(I)/30));
					JurosatePMT(I)   =SaldoAntesPMT(I)-SaldoInicioMes(I);
					if tipoentrada='NV'  then
						do;
							VLPMT(I) =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane1-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane11 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;
					else
						do;
							VLPMT(I)     =PMT(novataxa,(prazo_remane-j),SaldoAposLA(I-1));
							if prazo_remane22 =I then
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=0;
							else
								SaldoAposPMT(I)=SaldoAntesPMT(I)-VLPMT(I);
							SaldoAposLA(I)   =SaldoAposPMT(I)/(1+LA(I));
						end;

					SaldoFinalMes(I) =SaldoAposLA(I)*((1+novataxa)**(DiasAposPMT(I)/30));
					JurosaposPMT(I)  =SaldoFinalMes(I)-SaldoAposLA(I);
					if tpop = '05' then
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I))*0.9;
					else
						Receita(I)       =sum(JurosatePMT(I),JurosaposPMT(I));
				end;
			j=j+1;
		end;
end;
run;

data BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
set BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
safracontrato2=substr(DtContr,1,7);
run;

		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_38)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_38,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_39)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_39,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_40)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_40,
		  (SUM(t1.VLPMT_41)) AS SUM_of_VLPMT_41

      FROM WORK.BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3 t1
      GROUP BY t1.Data_Base,
               t1.tipoentrada,
               t1.Mod,
               t1.TPOP,
               t1.DS_OP;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	SELECT COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Min_Saldo,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.)) AS Max_Saldo,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_Receita,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_Receita_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_Receita,

		   COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MIN(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.))+1,2.)) AS Min_PMT,
           COMPRESS('SUM_of_VLPMT_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(TRANWRD(name,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)),2.))   AS Max_PMT

		INTO: Min_Saldo,
		    : Max_Saldo,
			: Min_Receita,
		    : Max_Receita,
			: Min_PMT,
		    : Max_PMT

	FROM dictionary.columns
	WHERE libname= upcase("WORK") AND memname = upcase("QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31") AND name LIKE "SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_%" escape '#';
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
SELECT DISTINCT MIN(INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0)) AS DataRef
	INTO: Data_Ref
FROM BASE_NOVOS_PARCE3;
QUIT;

DATA TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Saldo.--&Max_Saldo.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_Receita.--&Max_Receita.);
RUN;

DATA TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3;
	SET QUERY_FOR_BASEPARCE_NOVOS_31 (keep=Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op &Min_PMT.--&Max_PMT.);
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1 OUT=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2 OUT=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_Receita_:;
RUN;

PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3 OUT=TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP (RENAME=(COL1=Valor _NAME_=NmLinhaIndicador));
	BY Data_Base tipoentrada Mod TpOp DS_Op ;
	VAR SUM_of_VLPMT_:;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_SaldoFinalMes_',''),BEST12.) as Adicional,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   &Data_Ref. as Data format date9.,
		   
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,
		   INTCK('MONTH',CALCULATED DataCtt,CALCULATED DataRef) AS Mob_Contrato,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Saldo Carteira Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Saldo Carteira Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE

	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_Receita_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   &Data_Ref. as Data format date9.,
		   
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,
		   INTCK('MONTH',CALCULATED DataCtt,CALCULATED DataRef) AS Mob_Contrato,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'Receita Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'Receita Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'Receita Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'Receita Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'Receita Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP_1 AS

	SELECT a.*,
		   INTNX('MONTH',&Data_Ref.,INPUT(TRANWRD(a.NmLinhaIndicador,'SUM_of_VLPMT_',''),BEST12.)) FORMAT DATE9. AS DataRef,
		   YEAR(CALCULATED DataRef)*100 + MONTH(CALCULATED DataRef) AS SafraRef,
		   &Data_Ref. as Data format date9.,
		   
		   INTNX('MONTH',MDY(MOD(Data_Base,100),1,ROUND(Data_Base/100,1)),0,'e') FORMAT DATE9. AS DataCtt,
		   INTCK('MONTH',CALCULATED DataCtt,CALCULATED DataRef) AS Mob_Contrato,

		   CASE WHEN a.TpOp  IN ('0F')                              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Compulsorio')              THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Comp.'
                WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Fatura Convencional','Crediário') THEN 'PMT Parcelado Fatura Conv.'
		        WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Emissor')                         THEN 'PMT Parcelado Emissor'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo Amigavel')                 THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo Amig.'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Acordo CL')                       THEN 'PMT Parcelado Acordo CL'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('CP')                              THEN 'PMT Crédito Pessoal'
				WHEN a.DS_OP IN ('Conta')                           THEN 'PMT Parcelado PagContas'
				ELSE 'Verificar'
				END AS NmLinhaDRE
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP AS a;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE TBL_OUTPUT_PISMO_MODELOSAS AS

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_1_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)*(b.Qtd_DiasContabeis/30)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_2_TRANSP_1 AS a
	LEFT JOIN CARTAOBV.TBL_DIASCONTABEIS_CARTOES AS b
	ON a.SafraRef = b.MovRef
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0

	UNION ALL

	SELECT a.SafraRef,
	       a.Data_Base AS SafraCtt,
		   a.DataRef,
		   a.DataCtt,
		   a.Mob_Contrato,
		   a.DS_OP,
		   a.TipoEntrada,
		   a.NmLinhaDRE,
		   SUM(COALESCE(a.Valor,0)) AS Valor
	FROM TBL_APOIO_PISMO_3_TRANSP_1 AS a
    GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8
	HAVING Valor NE 0
;
QUIT;

data base3040;
set estudos2.CARTAO3040_&Safra_Referencia.; 
format safracontrato ddmmyy10.;
format dtref DT_contrato DT_vencop ddmmyy10.;
dia=compress(substr(DtContr,6,2));
mes1=substr(put(Data_Base,z6.),5,2);
mes2=substr(DtContr,6,2);
mes3=substr(DtVencOp,6,2);

ano1=substr(put(Data_Base,z6.),1,4);
ano2=substr(DtContr,1,4);
ano3=substr(DtVencOp,1,4);

dtref       = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes1||'/'||ano1),ddmmyy10.);
DT_contrato = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes2||'/'||ano2),ddmmyy10.);
DT_vencop   = input(compress('01'||'/'||mes3||'/'||ano3),ddmmyy10.);

prazo=          intck('Month',DT_contrato,DT_vencop);
prazo_decorrido=intck('Month',DT_contrato,dtref);
prazo_remane   =intck('Month',dtref,DT_vencop);

contratoparcelado=substr(Contrt,5,40)*1;
mes=substr(DtContr,6,2);
ano=substr(DtContr,1,4);
dia=substr(DtContr,9,2);
safracontrato=input(compress(dia||'/'||mes||'/'||ano),ddmmyy10.);
cdproduto1=cdproduto*1;
TPOP=substr(Contrt,3,2);
if TPOP='04' then DS_OP='Pagamento de Contas'; else
if TPOP='01' then DS_OP='Rotativo'; else
if TPOP='02' then DS_OP='Saque'; else
if TPOP='06' then DS_OP='Fatura Convencional'; else
if TPOP='0A' then DS_OP='Fatura Convencional'; else
if TPOP='0C' then DS_OP='Crediário'; else
if TPOP='05' then DS_OP='Acordo Amigavel'; else
if TPOP='07' then DS_OP='Avista'; else
if TPOP='08' then DS_OP='Limite'; else
if TPOP='0E' then DS_OP='Conta'; else
if TPOP='0B' then DS_OP='CP'; else
if TPOP='03' then DS_OP='Emissor'; else
if TPOP='0D' then DS_OP='Acordo CL'; else
if TPOP='0F' then DS_OP='Fatura Compulsorio';
if DiaAtraso <60 then Over= 'UnderOver60';else Over= 'Over60';
run;
